# 14 — Primary Source Locator and Claim-Revision Ledger

This is the provenance engine for the multi-document corpus. Each entry records a mature or migration-relevant claim, its audiovisual source locators, audit result, and primary analytical home.

**Permitted audit states:** `VERIFIED`, `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`, `QUALIFIED`, `REVISED`, `RETIRED`, `UNSUPPORTED`, `OPEN`.

**Current status:** Sequential claim provenance is populated through Episode 25. The targeted E01–E15 retrospective music/sound backfill is complete; Episodes 16–25 have been analyzed natively under v2. Episode 26 remains sealed.

---

# Retrospective v2 music/sound audit — Episode 1

The following claims were added after Episode 15 under `CinderellaGirlsAnalyticalMethod_v2.md`. They use **Episode 1 source only** and do not alter the original E01 prospective spoiler horizon.

## CG-MOTIF-0013 — E01 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** The cold open establishes clock/material-event sound before the sustained piano-led fairy-tale underscore, so the dream sound-world is superimposed on an already material city/event/production world rather than preceding it.

**Original analytical status:** E01 labor/magic coupling present visually; sonic ordering underweighted.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

**Primary evidence:**
- `E01-AUDIO00:00-00:09.57`
- `E01-T00:00-00:46.23`
- `E01-ASS0001-0013`
- `E01-FRAME_000004`
- `E01-FRAME_000007`
- `E01-FRAME_000010`
- `E01-FRAME_000019`
- `E01-CS01-02`

**Primary home:** `10_CINDERELLA_METAPHOR_VISUAL_ARCHITECTURE_TIME_SPACE_AND_MOTIFS.md`

**Secondary homes:**
- `01_SERIES_ARCHITECTURE_AND_EPISODE_PROGRESSION.md`

**Revision / audit note:** Strengthens `CG-CIND-0001`. Do not infer a fixed clock sound-motif or exact instrumental symbolism from one episode.


## CG-PERF-0014 — E01 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** The transition into `お願い！シンデレラ` at approximately 00:51 turns the suspended piano-led fairy-tale narration into a denser, regularly pulsed public performance with singing bodies and audience; the song's semantic field also places magic/dream language beside accumulation and `リアルなスキル`, strengthening the work/magic coupling.

**Original analytical status:** Cold-open performance recognized, but arrangement transition and musical embodiment underweighted.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

**Primary evidence:**

**Secondary homes:**

- `01_SERIES_ARCHITECTURE_AND_EPISODE_PROGRESSION.md`

**Revision / audit note:** The claim is dramaturgical, not a claim that exact harmony or key has symbolic meaning.


## CG-MOTIF-0014 — E01 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** The instrumental introduction of `メッセージ` begins immediately after Producer's final invitation and Rin's brief response, before the park-to-bedroom cut at 22:51.787, and continues across that public/private boundary; the song therefore functions as a sound bridge carrying an externally offered possibility into Rin's private deliberation before she chooses to act.

**Original analytical status:** Park decision and bedroom reflection understood visually/dialogically; continuous musical bridge not previously formalized.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

**Primary evidence:**

**Revision / audit note:** Strengthens Rin's agency by formalizing the private interval between invitation and assent. It does not claim that music causes the decision.


## CG-CIND-0009 — E01 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** Episode 1's opening and ending form a musical Cinderella bookend: suspended dream narration becomes embodied public performance in the opening, while `メッセージ` carries Rin from night reflection through dream/fantasy and morning-light song language into Cinderella Project paperwork/morning and her self-introduction; dream therefore becomes stage at the opening and chosen professional action at the ending.

**Original analytical status:** Materialist/procedural Cinderella model established; musical bookend not previously formalized.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

**Primary evidence:**

- `02_NEW_GENERATIONS_CHARACTERS_AND_RELATIONAL_SYSTEM.md`

**Revision / audit note:** Formal bookend only. This does not establish a shared leitmotif, a deterministic fate reading, or a fixed series-wide dream/light code.

# Retrospective v2 music/sound audit — Episode 11

The following claims were added after Episode 15 under `CinderellaGirlsAnalyticalMethod_v2.md`. They use **Episode 11 and earlier source only** and do not alter the original E11 prospective spoiler horizon.

## CG-PERF-0024 — E11 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** Episode 11 makes Miku and Riina's co-authorship audible by letting them voice incompatible cat/cute- and rock-centered draft language before `ΦωΦver!!` later contains both identity fields inside one shared song; the final unit sound is therefore produced through revision of failed one-sided drafts rather than presented as a fully formed institutional gift.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

## CG-REL-0031 — E11 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** The non-diegetic `We're the friends!` montage places Miku and Riina's songwriting labor inside a wider field of peer support and shared care, but the episode returns them to unresolved disagreement afterward; supportive community is therefore a developmental condition rather than a substitute author of compatibility.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

## CG-PERF-0025 — E11 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** The pre-song `にゃー` call-and-response changes from Miku-led cat-idol grammar and Riina's hesitant participation into a clear projected Riina response and repeated performer/audience call before the song begins; Riina can enter Miku's authored performance grammar without being assimilated into Miku's identity.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

## CG-PERF-0026 — E11 retrospective audio audit

**Claim:** `ΦωΦver!!` is heard as a completed public performance before the quiet office scene in which Miku and Riina explicitly ratify continuation and the provisional mark becomes `Asterisk`; Episode 11 therefore makes the unit musically/publicly legible before it becomes finally administratively named.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

---

# Native v2 sequential analysis — Episode 16

Episode 16 is the first sequential episode whose original canonical pass integrates the full v2 sound/voice method. The following claims use **E01–E16 semantic evidence only**; E17–E26 remain sealed.

**Claim:** E21 clarifies Mio's E20 solo decision without rewriting the E20 checkpoint: the solo move remains self-authored, but Mio explicitly links her crisis to a shaken belief that she should lead New Generations forward and to feeling unable to do anything when Rin's Triad desire appears; the solo work begins under leadership/relational pressure and then acquires additional developmental value through acting.

# Native v2 sequential claim delta — Episode 22

## CG-INST-0028 — E22

**Claim:** The Autumn Live is simultaneously public entertainment and a bureaucratic midterm survival test for Cinderella Project: Mio explicitly states that whether the winter ball can proceed will be decided here, while Producer deliberately tells the performers to set that evaluative pressure aside and orient toward enjoying the stage with the audience.

- `会場のお客さんと一緒に 笑顔で楽しんでください`

- `09_FAILURE_CARE_AUTONOMY_AND_DEVELOPMENTAL_RISK.md`

# Native v2 sequential claim delta — Episode 23

The following claims were created during the canonical Episode-23 native-v2 reading. They use **Episodes 1–23 only** as semantic evidence; Episodes 24–26 remained sealed.

## CG-PROD-0047 — autonomy is revisable when outcomes reveal avoidance

**Claim:** Producer initially honors Uzuki's self-request to suspend work and redo basic training because it appears potentially necessary for forward movement, but later explicitly says `このままでは良くない` and commits to repeated conversation; respecting performer autonomy therefore includes revising a prior judgment when observed consequences show that the chosen strategy is no longer functioning developmentally.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

**Evidence:**
- `E23-T00:46.78-01:36.58`, `E23-ASS0015-0028`, `E23-CS01-02`
- `E23-T03:18.01-03:24.85`, `E23-ASS0051-0052`, `E23-CS08`
- `E23-T10:57.80-11:56.82`, `E23-ASS0186-0201`, `E23-CS17`

**Key Japanese:**
- `ご本人の意向を汲むことにしました`
- `前に進むために必要ならと了承しました`
- `ですがこのままでは良くないと 今は思っています`

**Primary home:** `03_TAKEP_PRODUCER_DEVELOPMENTAL_AUTHORSHIP_AND_RESPONSIBILITY.md`

---

## CG-CHAR-0055 — Uzuki translates an identity crisis into technical deficits

**Claim:** Uzuki responds to her deteriorating self-conception by converting it into measurable training problems—stamina, fundamentals, additional steps and more practice—and explicitly says the training school feels calming; the strategy is sincere and technically productive in local terms, but narrows a question of individual value into a domain where effort always supplies an answer.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

**Evidence:**
- `E23-T01:09.97-01:36.58`, `E23-ASS0022-0028`, `E23-CS02`

- `E23-T09:29.22-10:38.53`, `E23-ASS0160-0179`, `E23-CS15-16`

**Key Japanese:**
- `もっと体力つけて`
- `基礎レッスンをやり直した`
- `ここに来ると何だか落ち着きます`
- `もっともっといろんなステップを こなせるように頑張ります`

**Primary home:** `02_NEW_GENERATIONS_CHARACTERS_AND_RELATIONAL_SYSTEM.md`

**Secondary home:** `09_FAILURE_CARE_AUTONOMY_AND_DEVELOPMENTAL_RISK.md`

---

**Claim:** Uzuki's temporary withdrawal generates concrete substitution and scheduling labor—Kanako replaces her beside Miho while Producer and the wider department redistribute work—yet Cinderella Project continues functioning through differentiated coverage; E23 makes both the cost of individualized care and the resilience of a plural production ecology visible.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

---

## CG-CHAR-0056 — Uzuki's crisis is comparative non-specificity rather than loss of idol desire

**Claim:** Uzuki explicitly says that others have found `キラキラ` through song, acting, dance and other challenges while she cannot identify `私の中のキラキラするもの`; she fears arriving at the deadline as the only one who has found nothing, so the E23 crisis is best grounded as comparative inability to name differentiated value, not as evidence that she never wanted to be an idol.

**Audit result:** `VERIFIED_STRENGTHENED`

- `私の中のキラキラするもの 何だか分からなくて`
- `もし私だけ何にも見つからなかったら`
- `怖いよ`

**Primary home:** `02_NEW_GENERATIONS_CHARACTERS_AND_RELATIONAL_SYSTEM.md`

## CG-CIND-0022 — `M@GIC☆` offers performed meanings of magic without closing the metaphor

**Claim:** `M@GIC☆` names `想い`, `気持ち`, inner light, effort and `絆` as magic, but the final narration later retains `魔法が何なのかまだ分からない`; the song therefore supplies performer-level experiential knowledge rather than a single exhaustive metaphysical definition.
